Basis: [`spec/02-logic-and-formal-errors.md` §A.4](../spec/02-logic-and-formal-errors.md#a4-fill-quantifiers-group-scopes-and-the-two-iteration-ranges) and [`spec/09-computations.md` §3.2](../spec/09-computations.md#32-an-invalidity-clear-poisons); pinned kernel 30.8.1 `RuntimeController`, `CalculationCache`, `CalculationCommand`, and generated computation-alternative catch path at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; read-only a12-dmkits ordered scans and maintained `ComputePoisonReadDiffTest`/`BareGroupComputeExactnessDiffTest` at revision `d90082aaeb15372b0ef7f593149967f02c28a790`, together with `ComputationFillQuantifierRestartDiffTest`, which is the test that actually separates the critical `[uninstantiated, filled, poison]` versus `[filled, poison, uninstantiated]` ordering and was omitted from this citation until the Batch B triage found it — the evidence was stronger than the basis line claimed; red/green range, threshold, composite, and eager-suffix mutations; independent semantic and Lean design reviews; trusted decision-prefix and read-order laws.

The seven predicates cannot be recovered from one validation tally or one kind-level empty rule. Computation turns an invalid slot into poison only when the ordered scan reaches it, while validation counts the same invalid cell in neither fill bucket. `AllFieldsFilled` and `NotAllFieldsFilled` inspect the declared range, so a declared-but-uninstantiated slot is a clean deciding absence. `NoFieldFilled`, `AtLeastOneFieldFilled`, `MoreThanOneFieldFilled`, and `NotExactlyOneFieldFilled` inspect instantiated slots only. `FieldsNotCollectivelyFilled` requires both an instantiated filled witness and a declared empty witness. Every operator terminates at its own deciding prefix; a malformed suffix after that prefix is semantically unread.

The two nonprimitive predicates are staged decisions, not Boolean aliases. `NotExactlyOneFieldFilled` first establishes whether any field is filled and, only then, seeks a second fill from a fresh source-level scan; zero and two-or-more hold, exactly one does not. `FieldsNotCollectivelyFilled` first seeks an instantiated fill and, only then, seeks a declared absence from a fresh scan. This yields the critical separator: `[uninstantiated, filled, poison]` holds because both witnesses are reached before the suffix, while `[filled, poison, uninstantiated]` poisons before the absence witness. A pure one-pass state machine could be extensionally equivalent over immutable resolved slots, but the chosen definition retains the source stages directly and makes no duplicate-read observation claim.

Lean treatment: [`Semantics/ComputationFillQuantifier.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/ComputationFillQuantifier.lean) defines the smallest honest resolved domain—`filled | empty | uninstantiated | poison cause`—and reuses only `ComputationConditionResult`. Three private source-order scans implement first fill, first declared absence, and second fill; the public closed enum dispatch keeps every language predicate explicit rather than inventing generic negation or a quantifier framework. [`Proofs/ComputationFillQuantifier.lean`](../A12Kernel/Proofs/ComputationFillQuantifier.lean) proves universal poison-head preservation, representative deciding-prefix suffix equations, the declared/instantiated range fork, zero/one/two classification, mixed-range success, selected reached-poison separators, and an order non-law. [`Conformance/ComputationFillQuantifier.lean`](../A12Kernel/Conformance/ComputationFillQuantifier.lean) locks the seven suffix-poison controls, exact internal causes before decisions, the uninstantiated-range matrix, both mixed-order separators, and zero/one/two cases. Eager suffix inspection failed eight cases; dropping uninstantiated declared slots failed three; stopping at the first filled slot failed four; collapsing `NotExactlyOneFieldFilled` into `MoreThanOneFieldFilled` failed two; and collapsing `FieldsNotCollectivelyFilled` into `NotAllFieldsFilled` failed its all-empty separator before exact restoration.

Limit: this begins with a caller-supplied marked stream after the future checked expansion of operands, groups, stars, declared omissions, and filters into field-major/repetition-major order. It does not derive that stream, read checked cells, evaluate `Having`, distinguish filtered versus absent provenance, build paths or repetition contexts, add a quantifier constructor to `ComputationCondition`, integrate with `And`/`Or` or alternatives, establish authored legality, project validation truth/polarity, run computations, expose protocol support, or retain project-local portable evidence. Exact cause carriage is an internal refinement; the maintained external differentials establish observable clean decision versus poisoned computation across both kernel routes and the peer evaluator, but correspondence remains `external evidence pending` here. The phase clarification and exact operator table are recorded by accepted successor [`SPEC-2026-07-20-12`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-20-12--computation-fill-quantifiers-retain-their-exact-staged-scans). No support, evidence, protocol, harness, governance mechanism, dependency, or generic quantifier framework was added.

## LF51 — validation field-fill quantifiers are extensional and observably collapsed

> Date: 2026-07-20. Sections: §1, §3, §12. Basis: [`spec/02-logic-and-formal-errors.md` §A.4](../spec/02-logic-and-formal-errors.md#a4-fill-quantifiers-group-scopes-and-the-two-iteration-ranges); pinned kernel 30.8.1 seven runtime quantifier methods and `ValidierungsErgebnis` at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; read-only a12-dmkits `quantifierFires` plus maintained malformed, field-list, group-scope, and filter-polarity differentials at revision `a2ee550943e5b6b43cd2316627ea8b94cf5e2b3c`; red/green unknown, range, composite, polarity, and decisive-witness mutations; independent semantic audit; and trusted witness, range, non-equivalence, and phase-separator laws.

Validation and computation share seven operator names but not one evaluation mechanism. Unfiltered validation depends extensionally on four counts: known filled instantiated cells, known empty instantiated cells, formally unavailable instantiated cells, and declared-but-uninstantiated omissions. An unavailable cell belongs to the instantiated range yet counts in neither fill bucket. A declared omission is empty only for the declared-range side. Consequently `NoFieldFilled` fires only when filled and unavailable are both zero, `NotAllFieldsFilled` fires from any instantiated or declared empty, and `FieldsNotCollectivelyFilled` needs both a known fill and a declared-range empty. `NotExactlyOneFieldFilled` has two firing regions with different polarities: zero known fills with no unavailable cell is OMISSION, while at least two known fills is VALUE.

The exact observable result is smaller than the project's general validation `Verdict`. The runtime exposes fired VALUE, fired OMISSION, or one `FALSE_OR_UNKNOWN`; neither source nor maintained differentials expose whether a silent quantifier was internally false or unknown. Lean therefore uses `ValidationFillOutcome.fired polarity | falseOrUnknown` instead of inventing a four-state refinement. This also blocks an unjustified validation read-order claim: the count evaluator promises the final observable classification only, whereas computation separately promises first-reached poison and deciding-prefix behavior.

Lean treatment: [`Semantics/FieldFillQuantifier.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/FieldFillQuantifier.lean) owns only the shared closed operator vocabulary. [`Semantics/ValidationFillQuantifier.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/ValidationFillQuantifier.lean) defines the four-count tally and seven explicit validation clauses; it shares no scan implementation with computation. [`Proofs/ValidationFillQuantifier.lean`](../A12Kernel/Proofs/ValidationFillQuantifier.lean) proves clean sufficient-witness laws, unknown-as-neither, universal instantiated-range independence from declared omissions, the declared/instantiated fork, `NotExactlyOneFieldFilled`'s polarity change, the mixed-versus-`NotAll` non-law, and the validation-collapse versus computation-poison separator. [`Conformance/ValidationFillQuantifier.lean`](../A12Kernel/Conformance/ValidationFillQuantifier.lean) locks the separating fill-count matrix, decisive witnesses despite unknown siblings, declared omissions, and the phase split. Twelve mutations failed exactly at the intended boundaries: unknown-as-empty for `No`, lost declared omission for `NotAll`, both composite collapses, wrong at-least-two polarity, incorrectly requiring unknowns to disappear before `AtLeastOne` fires, unknown admitted by `All`, unknown miscounted as an empty by `NotAll` or the mixed predicate, unknown admitted by the zero branch of `NotExactlyOne`, and uninstantiated omissions miscounted as fills by `AtLeastOne` or `MoreThanOne`.

Limit: the tally is caller-supplied after checked operand expansion, field classification, group/star range construction, and any filter selection. It does not establish range construction, physical reads, a hidden false/unknown distinction, `Having` provenance and polarity, empty-row eligibility, partial-validation relevance, path resolution, condition/connective integration, messages, protocol support, or project-local portable evidence. Maintained kernel differentials are sufficient to choose and triangulate the account, but this repository's correspondence remains `external evidence pending`. Accepted [`SPEC-2026-07-20-13`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-20-13--validation-fill-quantifiers-expose-an-extensional-collapsed-result) owns the canonical phase clarification. No support, evidence, protocol, harness, governance mechanism, dependency, or quantifier framework was added.

## LF52 — power fillability is a conservative branch table, not parity inference

> Date: 2026-07-20. Sections: §5 and §12. Basis: [`spec/04-numbers-and-decimals.md` §3](../spec/04-numbers-and-decimals.md#3-arithmetic-domain-failures-are-consumer-sensitive) and [`spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md` §4](../spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md#4-the-directional-fill-machinery-behind-the-typing); pinned kernel 30.8.1 staged power and `powCanChange*` source at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; read-only a12-dmkits `ArithFill.power`, branch controls, and dual-kernel-route zero-base/empty-exponent discriminator at accepted revision `71775c9905b057831253348c31ce39e321e61889`; red/green branch cases, seven mechanism mutations, independent semantic and Lean reviews, and trusted absorption/domain/zero-base laws.

Power value and power polarity are coupled but not derived from one formula. Value evaluation first enforces the integral `−1000..1000` domain, rejects zero to a negative exponent, applies the staged positive algorithm, and uses a precision-50 reciprocal before a negative exponent. Only a successful value receives directional metadata. For a nonnegative exponent the metadata dispatches first on operand fixedness, then on exponent zero/parity or the fixed base's region relative to `−1`, `0`, and `1`; the remaining both-variable case has one guarded grow-only region and otherwise returns both directions. This is the kernel's deliberately conservative approximation, not exact mathematical reachability and not a parity shortcut.

A negative exponent reuses that same nonnegative table only after two independent transformations: the base amount becomes the precision-50 reciprocal and its directions pass through the existing sign-sensitive reciprocal transform, while the exponent's grow/shrink directions are swapped. Domain invalidity wins before comparison. The most compact discriminator is therefore surprising but stable: `0 ^ emptyUnsigned` and `0 ^ emptySigned` both have current amount `1`; the former is shrink-only and makes `> 0` fire OMISSION, while the latter is grow-only and makes it fire VALUE. The same unsigned empty exponent gives grow-only at a fixed base above one and shrink-only at a fixed base between zero and one, so equal current results do not determine polarity.

Lean treatment: [`Semantics/NumericFillability.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/NumericFillability.lean) extends the existing `NumericArithmeticOutcome` with one `power` operation. It reuses `powerNumeric` as the sole amount/domain gate, the existing sign-sensitive reciprocal-direction transformation, and private branch helpers for the closed nonnegative table; no new result type or parallel arithmetic evaluator exists. [`Proofs/NumericFillability.lean`](../A12Kernel/Proofs/NumericFillability.lean) proves left/right unavailability absorption, exact agreement of outcome availability with `powerNumeric`, rejected-exponent suppression, universal fixed zero exponent, and the central equal-amount/different-direction pair. [`Conformance/NumericFillability.lean`](../A12Kernel/Conformance/NumericFillability.lean) covers every fixed-exponent, fixed-base, and both-variable table region, negative reciprocal and exponent-direction transformations, representative invalid domains, and downstream comparison polarity. The first draft's odd-exponent case used bidirectional base metadata and could not detect a parity collapse; one-directional positive and negative odd cases closed that test-mechanism gap.

Seven deliberate corruptions failed their predicted neighborhoods before exact restoration: collapsing the zero-base exponent-zero branch, omitting the negative-exponent direction swap, treating every fixed exponent as even, deleting the fixed-base short circuit for a fixed exponent, replacing the negative-base reciprocal transform with a plain direction swap, deleting the special both-variable grow-only branch, and turning invalid power into a fixed numeric value. LF34 subsequently connects the checked validation path to static exponent admission, the staged value, and this directional table; LF35 connects already-authored computation power to target invalidity and dependency poison. Concrete computation authoring, broader model/context construction, public protocol support, and retained project-local evidence remain open. Maintained sibling differentials choose and triangulate the value/fillability account but are not this repository's portable evidence, so correspondence remains `external evidence pending`. No support, evidence, protocol, harness, governance mechanism, dependency, or new arithmetic framework was added.

## LF53 — semantic-index lookup has match-first validation and column-first computation

> Date: 2026-07-20; amended 2026-07-23 and 2026-07-25. Sections: §3, §10, §11, and §12. **Locus correction (Batch C triage):** the phase asymmetry is *not* in `IndexFieldCache`, which this basis previously implied — that class has no phase notion at all and its lookup is total. The asymmetry is two local clause sequences in two different classes: validation resolves the match first and consults the column-wide check only inside its unknown-result branch, while computation's override checks the column and throws before delegating to the match. An auditor reading `IndexFieldCache` would find nothing and could wrongly conclude the claim unsupported. **Confirmed for the ordinary indexed-read route** by a maintained peer pair observing the exact discriminator on nearly the same document — a clean matching key coexisting with an unavailable key elsewhere in the column — firing under validation and clearing under computation on both kernel strategies. **The general form is not confirmed:** see the parallel-iteration limit at the end of this entry. Basis: [`spec/08-paths-and-references.md` §4](../spec/08-paths-and-references.md#4-rows-are-addressed-by-semantic-key-never-by-position-), [`spec/09-computations.md` §5](../spec/09-computations.md#5-where-a-computation-runs--scope-and-the-parallel-join), and the inherited [`spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md` §3](../spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md#3-every-fired-message-is-typed--value-vs-omission-); pinned kernel 30.8.1 `IndexFieldCache`, validation resolution, and computation cache gates at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; read-only maintained a12-dmkits indexed-read differentials at accepted revision `71775c9905b057831253348c31ce39e321e61889`; red/green cases, mechanism mutations, independent source/Lean/adversarial reviews, and trusted construction/gate/locality/empty/polarity laws.

Semantic-index lookup is not one generic “find row or fail” operation. After key normalization and index-field checking, validation first accepts a uniquely resolvable clean match and ignores an unrelated malformed, empty, or duplicate key entry. Only when no clean match exists does any unavailable key make the read UNKNOWN. Computation reverses those two gates: any unavailable key makes the entire indexed value read poison before it considers a clean match. Over a clean column, no matching row and a matched empty target both read `CellObservation.empty`; a selected formally invalid target is validation-unknown/computation-poison, while an invalid target in an unselected row is irrelevant.

Field role is load-bearing. An empty index key is auto-required and makes the index column unavailable to computation. The same `.required` finding on a selected ordinary target is ignored by computation observation and leaves that target empty, although validation observes it as unknown. A generic `CheckedCell` key scan would therefore be wrong: it would erase the index-key role and incorrectly apply the computation requiredness exception. The resolved column instead contains only unique clean canonical-token entries plus one optional unavailable-key cause. Duplicate participants, malformed keys, and empty keys are absent from the clean entries, and the requested literal token has already been checked nonempty. The low-level list lookup is total and first-match, but uniqueness is a preceding checked-layer obligation; incoherent duplicate clean entries receive no kernel-correspondence claim.

Key identity is also field-role-sensitive. `IndexFieldCache` normalizes both insertion and lookup only for a Number index declaration, so admitted decimal spellings collapse by numeric value; every non-Number key remains exact internally stored text. Numeric-looking String and Number keys must therefore occupy disjoint semantic-key constructors. a12-dmkits' reviewed document-owned map currently uses exact placement strings for every kind, so the Number-spelling separator is an outbound peer correction rather than inbound evidence.

Lean treatment: [`Semantics/SemanticIndex.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/SemanticIndex.lean) defines the canonical exact-text/Number key sum, compact resolved column, direct list reference lookup, two phase policies, and thin Number/presence/field-fill consumer projections. It adds no map, cache, path, row-address, or second evaluator. [`Elaboration/SemanticIndex.lean`](../A12Kernel/Elaboration/SemanticIndex.lean) supplies the first checked materialization route: one model-declared direct-child Number index, one Number target, one admitted literal or ordinary nonrepeatable Number field key, and one validated single-group raw context become the established resolved column after requiredness and duplicate staging. A dynamic key field uses the existing checked flat observation route; present Number values enter the literal lookup, empty performs the column's phase-aware no-match, and formal unavailability is rephased before selection. [`Semantics/NumericComparison.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/NumericComparison.lean) owns the shared observation-to-Number validation projection; [`Semantics/FlatValidation.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/FlatValidation.lean) and [`Semantics/ComputationCondition.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/ComputationCondition.lean) own their result-domain-specific observation-to-presence projections; and the existing validation/computation field-fill owners classify one observation as an extensional tally or ordered slot. Direct and indexed reads delegate to those consumers. The proof and conformance owners establish phase gates, selected-target locality, numeric/text non-collapse, checked-column delegation, topology rejection, required-key/required-target separation, duplicate exclusion, dynamic-key normalization/empty/failure, repeatable-key fail-closure, and downstream polarity/ordering behavior.

Eight original deliberate corruptions failed their predicted cases before exact restoration: putting validation's column gate first, putting computation's match first, turning clean no-match into unknown, selecting by physical list head instead of token, poisoning a required-only selected target, consulting an unavailable marker only for an empty entry list, making empty Number fixed, and turning a matched empty target into unknown. The first 2026-07-23 construction extension additionally separates Number value equality from same-looking text, duplicate participants from first-row selection, empty index requiredness from ordinary target emptiness, malformed topology from row scanning, and a clean validation match from computation's unrelated invalid-column poison. The field-key extension then separates literal bypass from checked field observation, normalized `5`/`5.00` identity, clean empty-key no-match from unavailable-column phase policy, formal key failure from target failure, and ordinary nonrepeatable admission from a repeatable key that requires row context. The implemented checked route is still deliberately narrow: it does not admit non-Number raw keys, repeatable field-valued keys, nested or multiple index levels, partial relevance, concrete syntax, protocol exposure, or an optimized lookup map. The maintained kernel routes establish the existing phase/presence/quantifier behavior and generic field-key shape, while the exact Number spelling separator awaits the pending a12-dmkits reconciliation; project-local portable correspondence remains pending. No support, evidence, protocol surface, harness, governance mechanism, dependency, or index framework was added.


**Parallel-iteration limit (Batch C triage, 2026-07-25).** The column-first computation gate is confirmed only for the *ordinary* indexed-read route. The computed-field template branches on parallel iteration, and on that branch there is no read-time gate at all: the iteration-values lookup returns an empty list so the loop body never executes, and a post-loop call marks affected instances afterwards — the kernel's own comment allowing that it "might be marking more field instances as invalid than strictly necessary." So a route-selected *mechanism substitution* sits underneath a claim stated uniformly, and the two accounts differ observably: with two sibling parent rows where only one carries a malformed key, the read-time account leaves the clean sibling's target computed while the post-loop marking account permits it to be cleared. No fixture in either repository sets up sibling parents under a parallel-iterated index group, so nothing currently observes it. Registered as [`EXP-2026-07-25-03`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#exp-2026-07-25-03--the-semantic-index-computation-gate-may-be-route-selected-not-read-time); until it closes, the Lean account's uniform read-time poison is scoped to the ordinary route.
## LF54 — Enumeration categories are positional runtime projections, not display lookups

> Date: 2026-07-20. Section: §8. Basis: [`spec/06-strings-and-enumerations.md`](../spec/06-strings-and-enumerations.md); pinned kernel 30.8.1 Enumeration declaration/checking, positional category construction, validation-cache lookup, runtime comparison, and polarity source at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; read-only maintained a12-dmkits Enum/category laws, focused differential, catalog smoke case, comparability locks, model loader, and peer runtime at revision `c418892b4b005095830bec557ba9a8162d4cae9d`; red/green cases, ten mechanism mutations, independent source/evidence/Lean reviews, and trusted exact/positional/many-to-one/empty/unavailable/VALUE-only laws.

An ordinary Enumeration has two distinct runtime projections. Direct access compares the stored token exactly and never substitutes its display label. Category access finds that stored token's declaration-order position and takes the token at the same position in one already-selected category. Category tokens need not be unique, so two different stored values can intentionally compare equal through a many-to-one category. Vector order is therefore semantic; treating a category as a set or sorting either vector changes results.

This runtime account begins after the static layer has admitted a legal closed Enumeration, a declared category, and a nonempty literal from the selected projection's domain. The declaration supplies unique nonempty stored tokens and exactly one nonempty category token per stored token. For admitted clean input, direct or category equality can fire only as VALUE; inequality is its separate exact operator, not verdict negation. Empty input is not evaluated under either operator, and formal unavailability remains UNKNOWN. A normal token mismatch is ordinary `notFired`, while a missing positional mapping is unreachable for a legal admitted model and remains only a defensive fail-closed UNKNOWN refinement.

Lean treatment: [`Semantics/ScalarEquality.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/ScalarEquality.lean) extracts the existing small exact equality/inequality evaluator without assigning a generic empty policy. [`Semantics/Enumeration.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/Enumeration.lean) owns the two resolved projections, lockstep list lookup, observation classification, and literal consumer. [`Proofs/Enumeration.lean`](../A12Kernel/Proofs/Enumeration.lean) proves exact head selection, pairwise skip, stored and category resolution, many-to-one verdict equality, empty and unavailable outcomes, and that a successfully resolved clean token cannot fire OMISSION. [`Conformance/Enumeration.lean`](../A12Kernel/Conformance/Enumeration.lean) separates positional order, many-to-one mapping, equality/inequality, empty, unavailable, wrong-kind, and incoherent input. Its stored-versus-display and stored-versus-category-token cases deliberately use out-of-domain literals as total-runtime controls and carry no authored-kernel correspondence claim.

Ten deliberate corruptions failed their predicted cases before exact restoration: selecting the next category token at a matching head, advancing only the stored-token vector during a non-head search, rejecting duplicate category tokens, substituting a present empty string for an empty cell, flattening unavailable input to not-evaluated, accepting String as Enumeration, making inequality equal equality, returning the stored token for category access, marking clean projection as not-given, and flattening projection miss to not-evaluated. The maintained focused nine-case differential uses the Groovy-dynamic kernel route plus peer triangulation; generated Java currently supplies only a broader catalog smoke case, and this repository retains no portable Enumeration observation. Correspondence remains `external evidence pending`.

Display-text and field-to-field comparability are a separate static-semantic problem, not a consequence of this runtime capsule; [`LF55`](#lf55--enumeration-field-comparability-depends-on-effective-remapping-not-label-presence) closes its ordinary resolved decision without coupling it to runtime projection. Open/extensible/dynamic enumerations, locale/display transport, authoring diagnostics, paths, value-list/category integration, semantic indices, checked model lowering, protocol support, and computation-phase category reads remain outside. No support, evidence, protocol, harness, governance mechanism, dependency, or general Enumeration framework was added.

## LF55 — Enumeration field comparability depends on effective remapping, not label presence

> Date: 2026-07-20. Section: §8. Basis: corrected [`spec/06-strings-and-enumerations.md` B.1](../spec/06-strings-and-enumerations.md#b1-comparison-is-by-stored-value-and-direct-field-comparability-depends-on-effective-display-remapping); pinned kernel 30.8.1 `IEnumerationType.hasDisplayValues`, `FieldUtils.isEnumWithDisplayValues`, `CheckVergleichsBedingungImpl`, and `CheckEntityListenUtils.checkDisplayValues` at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; read-only a12-dmkits model/comparability audit at revision `99f70a23a5bc392aafda05600b4d800848bab513` and accepted focused law coverage at revision `20230e403fa085c782534025f890669a975999a8`; red/green cases and mechanism mutations; independent source, Lean-design, and adversarial reviews; and trusted identity, conflict, rejection, and symmetry laws.

The static rule does not ask whether labels were authored. It asks whether any localized display actually differs from its stored token. An enum with no labels and one whose labels are all identity mappings therefore occupy the same effectively textless class and can be compared directly with an ordinary plain String field. A genuinely remapped enum cannot. Two enum fields must first agree on that class; if both are display-bearing, every locale common to both must preserve a partial bijection: one shared stored token cannot have two displays, and one shared display cannot denote two stored tokens. Their declarations need not be equal, so disjoint mappings and noncommon locales remain compatible.

[`Elaboration/EnumerationComparability.lean`](../A12Kernel/Elaboration/EnumerationComparability.lean) keeps this as an independent static decision rather than extending the flat runtime model. Each legal ordinary enum supplies locale-tagged stored/display facts; identity facts remain in the relation because another remapped member can make the whole profile display-bearing. The result distinguishes display-class mismatch from display-map conflict, and one Boolean projection exposes admission to future checked consumers. [`Proofs/EnumerationComparability.lean`](../A12Kernel/Proofs/EnumerationComparability.lean) proves identity-only profiles textless, pair and profile conflict symmetry, exact String admission, both rejection gates, and universal admission symmetry. [`Conformance/EnumerationComparability.lean`](../A12Kernel/Conformance/EnumerationComparability.lean) separates identity labels, genuine remapping, both conflict directions, an identity fact participating inside a display-bearing profile, compatible overlap, disjoint members, and noncommon locales.

Seven deliberate corruptions failed their predicted cases before restoration: reversing effective-display identity, ignoring locale, dropping the reverse display-to-stored constraint, suppressing the enum-class gate, suppressing map conflicts, accepting every String/Enumeration pair, and filtering identity facts out of a display-bearing profile's consistency relation.

This capsule begins after equality/inequality and direct-field shape checking, ordinary plain String admission, and legal ordinary closed-enum declaration checking. It assumes canonical locale keys, complete maps, unique stored tokens, and injective displays per locale. Table, open/dynamic, partial, and duplicate-display enums are excluded, as are arbitrary String expressions, literals, categories, exact outward diagnostic ordering, checked `FlatModel` integration, protocol support, and local portable evidence. Accepted [`SPEC-2026-07-20-14`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-20-14--enumeration-direct-field-comparability-uses-effective-display-remapping) records a12-dmkits revision `20230e403fa085c782534025f890669a975999a8`, whose existing legality harness now isolates identity labels, both map-conflict directions, identity participation in a display-bearing conflict, and compatible partial/disjoint mappings. This peer closure does not add a project-local portable observation, so correspondence remains `external evidence pending`. No support, evidence, protocol, harness, governance mechanism, dependency, or generic Enumeration framework was added.

## LF56 — `CustomCondition` purity preserves reproducibility, not locality

> Date: 2026-07-20. Sections: §11 and §14. Basis: [`spec/11-messages-and-custom.md` §4](../spec/11-messages-and-custom.md#part-b--14-customcondition--the-escape-hatch); pinned kernel 30.8.1 generated leaves, controller, cache, and Java/TypeScript wrappers at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; read-only maintained a12-dmkits dual-kernel-route, JVM, and Node invocation controls at revision `29b697c6ff0968fe9d575f9516cdd585a1ee51e1`; red/green invocation cases, three mechanism mutations, independent kernel/a12-dmkits/Lean-design audits, and trusted result/non-law theorems.

A reached custom-condition callback receives four conceptually distinct inputs: an effective document-wide data view, a full-validation marker or partial relevant-entity container, the complete formal-invalid-address payload for the validation run, and the current error pointer, which may be only partially known during parallel iteration. The evaluator does not pre-suppress the callback because formal-invalid addresses exist. Callback true fires as VALUE; false is the resolved not-fired result; the leaf is structurally empty-row eligible. Missing registration and host exceptions are integration failures, not callback false.

Lean keeps the four channels as independent opaque carrier types in [`Semantics/CustomCondition.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/CustomCondition.lean). This avoids falsely equating the host data view with the narrower internal `Document`, partial relevance with `FlatRelevance`, formal-invalid addresses with per-cell findings, or a partially-known error pointer with `CellAddr`. The pure total oracle is a direct argument to `evalReachedCustomCondition`, not a function stored in `World` or a registry embedded in the condition AST. [`Proofs/CustomCondition.lean`](../A12Kernel/Proofs/CustomCondition.lean) proves the exact two-result boundary and demonstrates two nearest non-laws: a pure callback may inspect arbitrary document data outside authored references, and it may react to the formal-invalid payload in either direction. Locality, monotonicity, read footprint, call count, and effect laws therefore require a separate callback contract and cannot follow from purity.

The capsule starts after successful name resolution and ordinary row/connective gating. It does not model concrete Java/Node data APIs, display/USB fidelity, relevance and pointer construction, parallel invocation mechanics, stateful callbacks, computation or `Having` rejection, message emission, checked lowering, protocol support, or project-local portable evidence. Maintained external controls are sufficient to choose the bounded account, but local correspondence remains `external evidence pending`. No support, evidence, protocol, harness, governance mechanism, dependency, host runtime, or generic oracle framework was added.

## LF57 — numeric absolute value changes directional provenance at zero

> Date: 2026-07-20; extended 2026-07-23. Sections: §5 and §10. Basis: [`spec/04-numbers-and-decimals.md` §3](../spec/04-numbers-and-decimals.md#3-arithmetic-domain-failures-are-consumer-sensitive), [`spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md` §4](../spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md#4-the-directional-fill-machinery-behind-the-typing); pinned kernel 30.8.1 value-function, comparison, directional-fill, wrapper checker, operation grammar, and legacy authoring-walk source at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; read-only maintained a12-dmkits `Abs` evaluation, fillability, computation-domain, nested-wrapper, and wrapper-over-extremum controls through exact reviewed arithmetic-wrapper revision `f9e591a54aa4c319baa42dfb76610d7571be5ecc` plus revision `27afb555aae29d3acd4ed04e3aea4772ae85505a`; red/green cases, focused mechanism mutations, independent family and proof reviews, and trusted magnitude, availability, sign-branch, static-summary, lowering, totality, computation-delegation, and validation-delegation laws.

Absolute value changes more than the current amount. Its magnitude is the standard rational absolute value, but its directional metadata depends on the source amount before transformation: either source direction can grow the magnitude; below zero, source growth can shrink it toward zero; above zero, source shrinkage can shrink it; and at zero no source direction can shrink the result. This makes a signed empty Number a sharp discriminator. Direct `S >= 0` fires as OMISSION because a later negative fill could clear it, while `Abs(S) >= 0` fires as VALUE because the substituted zero magnitude cannot later become negative. Equal current amounts therefore do not identify equal validation polarity.

Lean extends the existing authored/lowered numeric expression rather than introducing an operation-specific tree. `Abs` is the second completed value-function consumer alongside rounding, so each already-distinct numeric result domain owns one small `mapValue` seam that transforms only its available branch and preserves not-evaluated, domain-failure, and exact poison/unknown causes. `Abs` specializes those maps with the sign-sensitive fillability table. The proof spine bridges the executable magnitude to Lean's standard `Rat.abs`, proves all three direction branches and generic non-value preservation, preserves the authored scale summary, delegates computation results and checked validation to the shared seams, and keeps unsupported evaluator fallbacks unreachable. The compact cases separate negative, positive, and zero directionality, left and right validation operands, formal invalidity, computation domain failure and poison, scale identity, root/enclosing arithmetic placement, wrapper order, and expression-valued-extremum consumption. Earlier rejection controls correctly guarded the then-closed source boundary; later source audits replaced them with positive nested-wrapper and list-preserving-extremum cases while retaining immediate-literal rejection. Mutating either zero or positive shrinkability, admitting that literal boundary, or converting a computation domain failure to zero fails the corresponding proof or case neighborhood.

The checked validation and computation boundaries admit `Abs` over an already-checked number-like child at the root or an ordinary arithmetic operand position and map its final outcome through the existing sign-sensitive transform. Immediate or grouped literals remain rejected, direct/grouped numeric `BaseYear` is admitted, and another wrapper or a list-preserving expression-valued extremum may be the child. Nested wrappers execute in authored order: `RoundDown(Abs(-1.4)) = 1`, while `Abs(RoundDown(-1.4)) = 2`. The child retains its own division, power, per-call extremum budget, target-reference, read-order, domain-failure, and poison behavior. The enclosing walk carries division contributions across every reached function subtree, but each function node separates an outer direct-left power. Expression/`AbsValue` parsing aliases, Date overloads, stars and filters, target scheduling, public protocol, and project-local portable evidence remain open. Correspondence is therefore `external evidence pending`; this extension adds no evidence, protocol, harness, governance mechanism, dependency, or parallel evaluator.

## LF58 — numeric operand-list extrema combine exact selection with directional fillability

> Date: 2026-07-20; extended 2026-07-23. Sections: §3, §5, §10, and §12. Basis: [`spec/03-empty-and-required.md`](../spec/03-empty-and-required.md), [`spec/04-numbers-and-decimals.md`](../spec/04-numbers-and-decimals.md), [`spec/09-computations.md`](../spec/09-computations.md), and [`spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md`](../spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md); pinned kernel 30.8.1 grammar, operand checker, parse-tree, code-generation, runtime fold, exact-selection, fillability, and origin source at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; read-only maintained a12-dmkits expression, fillability, computation-domain, typed list, and differential material through revision `27afb555aae29d3acd4ed04e3aea4772ae85505a`; red/green separating cases, focused mechanism corruptions, source and same-context closure audits, and trusted normalization, source-certificate lowering, selection, availability, tie-fillability, reached-result, and ordered-evaluation laws.

Numeric operand-list `Min` and `Max` are nonempty authored calls, not aggregates and not comparisons. Each immediate member is a complete number-like operation. One call's runtime body is a left-associated fold with no synthetic zero seed, but the source call boundary remains observable: the checker permits at most one immediate or bracketed literal per call, while a literal inside arithmetic or a nested call does not consume the outer budget. Thus `Min(Min(A, 1), 2)` is admitted and `Min(A, 1, 2)` is rejected even though flattening would preserve mathematical minimum. A singleton likewise retains a call boundary for authoring rather than collapsing to its operand. Each reached Number operand contributes its current amount, so an empty Number contributes the real fillable value zero and competes for selection. Selection compares exact values before the later scale-19 comparison boundary. The static result takes the maximum exact operand scale, while constant-style trailing-zero expansion remains available only if every operand supplies it. This differs deliberately from starred aggregate `MinValue`/`MaxValue`, which discard empty cells, and from the Date overloads.

The selected amount does not by itself determine validation polarity. For `Min`, either operand may shrink the result, growth follows the currently smaller operand, and both tied operands must be able to grow; `Max` mirrors this, with unconditional growth and selected-side shrinkage. Computation adds an independent order rule: a reached left poison stops the suffix, but arithmetic domain failure remains a value-level result, so evaluation continues and a later reached poison wins. The second computation consumer exposed real duplication in the first draft. [`Semantics/NumericComputationResult.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/NumericComputationResult.lean) now owns one generic reached-result poison/domain/value table, and [`Elaboration/NumericComputation.lean`](../A12Kernel/Elaboration/NumericComputation.lean) owns one delayed-right ordered evaluator used by both arithmetic and extrema; the proof spine states those mechanism laws once rather than restating them per operator.

The checked validation, computation, and generated-validation routes now retain an explicit `extremumCall` around the existing internal binary fold. One shared source-shape scan validates arbitrary numeric operands and every nested call, classifies immediate/grouped literals, and rejects a second literal only within that call. Lowering caches this source-derived fact because multiplication lowering may change runtime topology but cannot change authoring history; the proof spine states that certificate preservation universally. One total numeric-operation evaluator consumes arithmetic, power, rounding, absolute value, raw fold nodes, and checked call markers, so the broader syntax does not introduce an extrema-specific evaluator. Cases distinguish grouped literals, arithmetic-contained literals, nested same/different calls, flat rejection, surrounding arithmetic, wrappers, checked computation, generated validation, target references, domain failure, and poison. Lean retains amount and directional fillability, not concrete source origins; therefore the kernel's equal-value origin choice—left for `Min`, right for `Max`—remains an explicit exclusion rather than a theorem inferred from the tie-fillability laws.

The original selection/fillability corruptions plus the later call-boundary, constant-budget, lowering-certificate, and composition controls fail their predicted proof or case neighborhoods. The numeric family is complete internally at levels 1–2 for represented nonrepeatable operands and remains project-local `external evidence pending`; accepted upstream overlap is recorded by `SPEC-2026-07-23-05`, while accepted [`SPEC-2026-07-23-06`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-23-06--numeric-operand-list-extrema-preserve-per-call-boundaries) and a12-dmkits revision `434f4ec8` supply the exact peer authoring locks. No evidence, protocol, harness, governance mechanism, dependency, source-origin model, aggregate framework, or parallel expression evaluator was added.

## LF59 — numeric aggregate extrema erase empty values but retain their polarity potential

> Date: 2026-07-20. Sections: §3, §5, §9, and §10. Basis: [`spec/03-empty-and-required.md` §A.3](../spec/03-empty-and-required.md#a3-what-an-all-empty-selection-folds-to-the-aggregate-identities), [`spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md` §4](../spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md#4-the-directional-fill-machinery-behind-the-typing), pinned kernel 30.8.1 `NumberCombiner` source at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`, read-only a12-dmkits aggregate implementation and controls at revision `06128aa0c96f36f34d6f5782c35d33e13001647e` plus accepted corrected implementation and tri-engine controls at revision `20230e403fa085c782534025f890669a975999a8`, red/green separating cases, five focused mechanism corruptions, independent semantic/proof review, and trusted availability, all-empty, tail, and filter laws. Accepted [`SPEC-2026-07-20-15`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-20-15--all-empty-number-aggregate-identity-is-both-directionally-fillable) records the peer correction.

Resolved Number `MinValue` and `MaxValue` differ deliberately from operand-list `Min` and `Max`. They scan every already-selected cell, drop empty cells from amount selection, stop on the first reached unavailable cell, and choose present values at exact precision without a synthetic zero seed. Zero appears only when no present value exists. That all-empty identity is conservatively both-directionally fillable even for unsigned fields and either operator; once a present value exists, missing cells or an uninstantiated tail make `MaxValue` grow-only and `MinValue` shrink-only.

Amount and polarity therefore remain separate observations. The same selected amount may be fixed for a complete selection, grow-only or shrink-only after missing potential, or both-directionally fillable after a reached `Having`; a filtered-empty selection is likewise zero with both directions. The later Sum capsule established a genuinely shared classified-cell scan for extrema and Sum, and the checked-row `NumberFold` now projects through that Sum mechanism. Extrema still do not share prefix-terminating `FirstFilledValue` or empty-substituting operand-list `selectOutcome`. Direct nonrepeatable unfiltered field lists now have checked lowering through the same source owner; path/star expansion, actual filter evaluation, partial relevance, computation aggregates, non-Number overloads, protocol exposure, and project-local portable evidence remain open.

## LF60 — Number Sum is an ordered staged fold with per-source missingness

> Date: 2026-07-21. Sections: §5, §9, and §10. Basis: [`spec/04-numbers-and-decimals.md` §5](../spec/04-numbers-and-decimals.md#5-internal-precision--the-constants-that-must-match-exactly), [`spec/07-repetition-and-iteration.md` §3](../spec/07-repetition-and-iteration.md#3-the-filter-having-the--correlation-and-aggregation), and [`spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md` §4](../spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md#4-the-directional-fill-machinery-behind-the-typing); pinned kernel 30.8.1 aggregate and decimal source at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; read-only a12-dmkits aggregate, decimal, polarity, and differential material at revision `baf70f0c252475d4a3f6c0f817f0451b5e52a185` plus accepted staged-fold and per-missing-declaration controls at revision `20230e403fa085c782534025f890669a975999a8`; red/green precision/order cases, independent source/Lean/matrix review, and trusted generic scan plus Sum identity, termination, signedness, and filter laws. Accepted [`SPEC-2026-07-21-02`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-21-02--number-sum-preserves-encounter-order-staged-precision-and-missing-declaration-polarity) owns peer reconciliation.

Number `Sum` is not mathematical addition over a set or an exact rational total. It is a zero-seeded left fold over the resolved encounter stream, and every present value—including the first—passes through precision-50 `HALF_UP` addition. The values `10^50`, `-10^50`, and `3/5` distinguish left from right association, while reordering that same multiset changes the staged result. Consequently a refactoring, parallel evaluator, database aggregate, or SMT encoding may not sort, reassociate, chunk, or exactly sum then round once unless it proves equivalence for a restricted operand domain.

Empty contributors take no arithmetic step but remain semantically observable through missingness. After at least one present value, Sum can always grow and can shrink exactly when at least one missing contributing declaration is signed; the all-empty zero remains both-directionally fillable regardless of signedness. Lean now retains declaration signedness on every selected source and separately on every uninstantiated source. This distinguishes an unsigned present plus signed missing source, which is both-directionally fillable, from a signed present plus unsigned missing source, which remains grow-only. The former homogeneous API is exactly the constant-signedness embedding into that richer side.

The implementation extracts only the branch structure shared with resolved extrema: scan left to right, skip empty cells, stop at the first unavailable cell, and apply a consumer-owned step to present cells. The stop-at-first-unavailable rider reads as a probed prefix-termination fact but is not one: a draining aggregate reaches the same failure set whether it stops at the first unavailable cell or drains and then poisons, so the *position* of the unavailable cell is knowingly unobservable, exactly as [`LF69`](#lf69--computation-traversal-order-can-be-real-while-its-failure-source-is-publicly-cause-blind) records for its own draining route. The cited malformed-aggregate differential cannot separate the two accounts. Read the rider as the implementation's chosen order, not as an externally established one. Generic scan laws are proved once; Sum and extrema retain separate accumulator, amount, and fillability laws. The old checked-row `NumberFold` delegates through the homogeneous embedding but remains a lossy amount-or-cause projection, so it does not claim the richer polarity semantics. [`Elaboration/NumericAggregate.lean`](../A12Kernel/Elaboration/NumericAggregate.lean) now closes one exact authored subset shared by all three operators: a nonempty unfiltered list of nonrepeatable Number paths. It resolves fields in authored order, retains each field's own signedness for Sum, checks raw cells with the same model, and constructs no tail or `Having` marker before delegating to the operator-specific resolved evaluator. A trusted law proves that the Sum and extremum views contain the same classified cells in the same order. Cases separate staged Sum order, both mixed-missing Sum directions, extremum-specific missing directions, all-empty identity, first formal unavailability, wrong kind, and repeatable rejection; trusted laws lock the exact no-tail/no-filter delegations. The family remains `external evidence pending`; group/star expansion, filters, partial relevance, legal-model high-precision calibration, protocol exposure, and broader consumer qualification remain open.

## LF61 — an empty observation does not imply an absent placement

> Date: 2026-07-21; extended through String presence and absolute requiredness. Sections: §1, §2, §3, and §4. Basis: [`spec/01-data-model.md` §2](../spec/01-data-model.md#2-document--an-instance-of-the-tree), [`spec/03-empty-and-required.md` A.1 and B](../spec/03-empty-and-required.md), pinned kernel 30.8.1 `DocumentUtils.valueExists` at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`, read-only a12-dmkits IF198 implementation and tri-engine controls at accepted revision `f78f4fc864b7be05f94736070cd2da7bf95d04b3`, red/green checked-cell/presence/required cases, and trusted placement, observation, presence, and staging laws. Accepted [`SPEC-2026-07-21-03`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-21-03--empty-string-ingestion-preserves-present-empty-placement) owns the cross-project reconciliation.

Physical placement and evaluation value are independent. An omitted cell and a placed cell containing raw `null` or `""` have no evaluation value and therefore supply the same ordinary empty observation, but the latter remains present-empty. Inferring placement from `Option Value` silently preserves many predicate results while losing information needed by exact document read-back, group-content derivation, and computation application.

Lean represents the distinction at the existing scalar boundary: `RawCell.empty` formal-checks to `rawPresent = false`, while `RawCell.presentEmpty` and a parsed empty String formal-check to `rawPresent = true, parsed = none`; all three are observed as `CellObservation.empty`. The checked-cell invariant states the generic direction that a parsed value requires placement without forbidding a clean present-empty cell. The proof spine establishes placement preservation, absent/present-empty inequality, equal phase observations, and parsed-empty-String normalization once. `FlatField.string` now lets `FieldFilled`/`FieldNotFilled` consume that observation through the existing generic presence evaluator, and checked flat lowering resolves String declarations into the same typed boundary. Absolute requiredness reuses the generated `FieldNotFilled` stage; its law proves that the mandatory hit attaches `.required` without changing a present-empty String's `rawPresent = true` placement.

The local capsule does not yet adapt the concrete `Document` into `RawCell`, derive non-repeatable or repeatable group content, implement repeatable/parent-gated requiredness, admit String guards in the separate generated-computation-validation fragment, invoke custom validators, model computation-applied copy behavior, or expose the distinction through the public protocol. Those consumers must reuse the preserved placement rather than reconstruct it from the empty observation. a12-dmkits supplies maintained external confirmation, but this repository retains no portable IF198 observation; local correspondence remains `external evidence pending`.

## LF62 — reopened-star completeness is hierarchical metadata, not a flattened count

> Date: 2026-07-21. Sections: §9 and §12. Basis: [`spec/07-repetition-and-iteration.md` §4](../spec/07-repetition-and-iteration.md#4-where-a-star-binds--the-anchor-rules-) and [`spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md` §4](../spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md#4-the-directional-fill-machinery-behind-the-typing); read-only a12-dmkits IF194 implementation and `MultiStarDeclaredTailPolarityDiffTest`/`StarOnlyIterationScopeDiffTest` controls at revision `7f152509eea76822068955055b0d57d8ed930ca2`; red/green shallow-count separators; and trusted finite-closure, unbounded, child-propagation, coordinate, and resolved-side bridge laws.

An aggregate tail cannot be derived from one flattened leaf count. Starting at the first star, every reopened repeatable level must meet its cap beneath every actual parent; a missing middle or leaf branch remains open even when another dense branch supplies enough flattened descendants. An unbounded reopened level is always open. Levels above the first star are absent from this structural decision because they remain bound. Selected-cell emptiness is a separate missingness input: the repetition tree determines only declared-but-uninstantiated structure, and `ResolvedValueListSide.hasMissingPotential` combines that result with empty classified cells.

Lean therefore adds [`Semantics/StarCompleteness.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/StarCompleteness.lean), a recursive first-star-relative domain that retains positive, sibling-unique actual row coordinates under their actual parents and never materializes a declared Cartesian product. The first shallow implementation passed outer-tail and unbounded cases but failed both missing-middle and missing-leaf cases; mutual recursion over child subtrees fixed the mechanism once. [`Proofs/StarCompleteness.lean`](../A12Kernel/Proofs/StarCompleteness.lean) characterizes finite closure and the exact resolved-side bridge, while [`Conformance/StarCompleteness.lean`](../A12Kernel/Conformance/StarCompleteness.lean) locks the outer/middle/leaf/full/bound/unbounded matrix, malformed coordinate controls, empty-leaf composition, and one existing Sum consumer.

The capsule begins after authored path checking, identification of the first star, model repeatability lookup, scoped `Document` row enumeration, and selected-cell classification. Those stages must construct the well-formed reopened tree and the ordered cell stream consistently; the current Lean theory does not yet do so. Aggregate operator directions, `Having`, partial relevance, per-source declaration-metadata construction, computation, messages, and public protocol support remain with their existing owners. The a12-dmkits tri-engine cases are external triangulation, not retained project-local evidence, so correspondence remains `external evidence pending`.

## LF63 — validation group presence is one state with intentionally different consumer projections

> Date: 2026-07-21. Sections: §3, §9, and §12. Basis: [`spec/03-empty-and-required.md` B.1](../spec/03-empty-and-required.md#b1-the-generated-rule-precisely), [`spec/07-repetition-and-iteration.md` §5](../spec/07-repetition-and-iteration.md#5-groupfilled-and-the-other-repetition-rules), and [`spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md` §5](../spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md#5-full-vs-partial-validation); read-only a12-dmkits IF193 product state, admission index, and tri-engine controls at revision `7f152509eea76822068955055b0d57d8ed930ca2`; red/green product-state separators; and trusted scalar, admission, partition, count, and requiredness laws.

Validation group presence cannot be reconstructed from raw placement, one content Boolean, or one shared count. Formally admitted content and propagated error are independent: malformed-only input is contentless and erroneous; a duplicate-index value or valid-plus-malformed input is both admitted and erroneous; an instantiated repeatable row is structural content even when empty or over-limit. Relevance is a third independent `NONE`/`PARTIAL`/`FULL` dimension because positive presence needs only a visible admitted witness while absence needs complete clean coverage.

Lean's [`Semantics/GroupPresence.lean`](../A12Kernel/Semantics/GroupPresence.lean) begins after descendant scope and relevance resolution. It folds existing `CheckedCell` values plus independently resolved row-content and structural-error facts into the product state, then gives scalar predicates, fixed-list predicates, the plain numeric count, and parent-filled requiredness their own projections. The central separator is deliberate: an admitted-but-erroneous group enters a group-list predicate's filled bucket, yet makes plain `NumberOfFilledGroups` unavailable. Sharing the state is correct; sharing one Boolean tally is not.

The capsule does not yet enumerate descendants from model paths, adapt `Document`, construct partial relevance from wildcardable patterns, expand repeatable group lists, or integrate generated mandatory rules with checked whole-rule orchestration. Those later stages must supply the resolved input without discarding admission order, structural rows, errors, or coverage. The a12-dmkits tri-engine cases are external triangulation rather than retained project-local evidence, so correspondence remains `external evidence pending`.

## LF64 — computed Number no-fit checks only the format prefix before decimal failure

> Date: 2026-07-21. Sections: §3, §5, and §11. Basis: [`spec/09-computations.md` §4](../spec/09-computations.md#4-the-stored-form--a-computed-value-lands-as-a-string-in-the-targets-shape); pinned kernel 30.8.1 `CalculationController.handleBerechnetenWert` and `FormatDefinitionZahl.validiereFormat` at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; read-only a12-dmkits `typeFormatErrorCached`/`numberConstraintError` at already-aligned revision `3194c1f73005fbf2c88538e2c2d9899fc695e09a`; red/green overlapping-failure cases; and trusted first-error dispatch laws.

The computed Number target has one shared prefix, not one undifferentiated constraint list. Total-digit overflow is checked before signedness. A fitting canonical attempt then reaches effective integer-digit capacity, zero prohibition, rendered minimum/maximum length, and inclusive numeric bounds. Its construction already satisfies the fractional interval and cannot produce plus, signed zero, invalid characters, or leading zeroes.

A warning-suppressed no-fit attempt diverges after the shared prefix. If total digits and signedness pass, its excess fractional shape fails before the fitting-only constraints; a zero/range/length violation therefore cannot replace that decimal cause. Conversely, total-digit or signedness failure remains more specific than the decimal mismatch. Lean models this with `firstAttemptError?` for the prefix and `firstFittingAttemptError?` for the complete fit path, so extending later target constraints cannot silently change no-fit precedence.

The policy carries an already-resolved effective integer cap: explicit `maxIntegerDigits` and the amount trait's derived `15 − maxFractionalDigits` have the same runtime error and need no separate target branch. The checked operation now attaches and retains that complete externally resolved policy once; construction from complete declaration metadata remains outside because `FlatFieldDecl` erases those constraints. No new a12-dmkits request is created because the peer already routes both input and computed values through the shared basic format checker. Composition triage 2026-07-25 closed both halves by reading, so no probe is required. **Reachability** is confirmed at the parser-check locus: `MVK_INVALID_COMPARE_DEC_PLACES` is a `ParserErrorTypesEnum` member carrying an authored suppression directive (`@UnterdrueckeWarnung`), and the lenient preamble checker records that it is currently the *only* suppressible diagnostic — so a no-fit computed attempt genuinely cannot arise in a legal model without that explicit authored suppression, and the branch is not dead. **Precedence** is a clause sequence decided inside the format checker, reached by an ordinary delegation rather than an interleaving of two runtime classes, so it is a local ordering fact that source reading establishes reliably and the hypothesis rule does not implicate. The local `NumericTarget` conformance matrix already observes the `firstAttemptError?`/`firstFittingAttemptError?` seam: an active integer-digit-capacity failure must not replace `.suppressedScaleMismatch`. That witness is sufficient to catch an accidental fitting-path call; zero, length, and range need no duplicate no-fit cases while they remain branches of that same skipped helper. No upstream kernel-executing case locks the precedence, so its remaining absence belongs to `Kernel-locked`, not local test coverage.

## LF65 — temporal comparison compatibility is intentionally coarser than aggregate compatibility

> Date: 2026-07-22. Sections: §6 and §12. Basis: [`spec/05-dates-and-time.md` §3](../spec/05-dates-and-time.md#3-constructing-dates-and-checking-validity); pinned kernel 30.8.1 `CheckVergleichsBedingungImpl`, `DateFormat`, and `CheckEntityListenUtils` source at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; read-only a12-dmkits temporal coverage audit at revision `3b9584602f5d7a7ed8b241d963aa37870fb1288c`; red/green component-set separators; and trusted symmetry and implication laws. Pending [`SPEC-2026-07-22-01`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-22-01--direct-temporal-comparison-and-extrema-use-different-format-gates) owns peer reconciliation.

The kernel does not have one universal “comparable temporal format” predicate. Direct comparisons deliberately use a coarse gate: after optional Base Year supplementation, year presence and date/no-date class must match; equality and inequality additionally require matching time/no-time class. Exact components do not otherwise matter, so `yyyy-MM < yyyy-MM-dd`, Date ordering against DateTime, and `HH:mm == HH:mm:ss` are statically admissible even though their resolved values may still differ.

Extrema use the stricter relation. Their component sets must be exactly equal after Base Year supplementation, and a DateTime extremum must expose all six components. Lean therefore retains one `TemporalComponents` structure but two named predicates rather than weakening aggregates or over-restricting comparisons. Exact aggregate compatibility implies every direct operator's gate, and equality admission implies directional admission, but neither converse holds. Parsing, concrete format strings, kind homogeneity, operand classification, zone resolution, and checked lowering remain separate; the capsule does not claim that every admitted static pair produces two values at runtime.

## LF66 — partial value-list relevance is per cell and per star extent, while only firing is completion-stable

> Date: 2026-07-22. Sections: §8 and §12. Basis: [`spec/06-strings-and-enumerations.md` §B.3](../spec/06-strings-and-enumerations.md#b3-the-value-list-quantifiers--per-cell-three-way-classification-) and [`spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md` §5](../spec/10-validation-and-polarity.md#5-full-vs-partial-validation); pinned kernel 30.8.1 `RuntimeController`, `ValidationCommand`, and `ValidationCache` value-list/partial-relevance paths at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; red/green flat String, Enumeration, and Number plus nested-star Number/String separators; and trusted full-specialization, masked-read, early-skip, extent, and firing-stability laws.

Partial validation does not gate a value-list leaf on every referenced field being relevant. It classifies each operand cell separately, removes nonrelevant cells before any value read, and preserves the fact that masking occurred for the quantifier's asymmetric rule. `AtLeastOne` skips masked cells on both sides and may still fire from a relevant match. `No` treats masking on either side as UNKNOWN. `NotAll` skips masked subject cells but treats a masked values-side member as UNKNOWN after a present subject exists. The former flat implementation instead returned UNKNOWN for the entire leaf as soon as any operand was nonrelevant; that accidentally erased relevant witnesses across String, Enumeration, and Number lists.

A starred entry additionally needs a complete-extent fact. Concrete relevance still decides which instantiated cells may be read, but only one covering identifier wildcarded at every reopened level, or an ancestor identifier that projects to one, proves that no absent/future row lies outside the partial slice. Listing every current row concretely is therefore not equivalent. This existential gate is deliberately not the four combiner aggregates' universal gate over normalized, redundancy-reduced, in-subtree identifiers; the two disagree when one covering field wildcard survives beside concrete group identifiers. Both are asked per current iteration row over the levels reopened from the first star. The same asymmetric dispatcher consumes the value-list extent fact: fields-side `AtLeastOne` and `NotAll` can still use relevant witnesses, while `No` is UNKNOWN; on a starred values side, `No` and `NotAll` are UNKNOWN.

This correction disproves a broader proof claim that partial truth always information-refines full truth. A partial `AtLeastOne` with no relevant match can be definitely non-firing and later fire when a masked matching cell is restored. The kernel's actual safety guarantee is one-directional: an error already fired by partial validation remains fired under a full completion that agrees on relevant reads, although its polarity may change. Conjunction and disjunction preserve that property because a partial firing supplies the required fired branch or branches; they do not require non-firing leaves to remain false.

Lean retains partial nonrelevance outside `ValueListCell`, whose UNKNOWN constructor continues to mean a formal cause. One shared classified quantifier evaluator now serves ordinary full sides, flat token and Number sides, and the checked nested-star Number/String routes without changing aggregate or prefix-scan inputs. The checked star-path owner filters concrete cells before their readers and supplies `!valueListExtentRelevant` as the distinct extent fact, while aggregate consumers use the separate `allRowsRelevant` predicate. The Number route also makes locally visible `Having` skip before topology and either reader, but a future whole-rule owner must discover `Having` across every branch before invoking any leaf. Other repeated kinds, side shapes, rule-wide orchestration, messages, protocol exposure, and project-local portable evidence remain open. The original extent correction was handed off under [`SPEC-2026-07-22-09`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-22-09--partial-starred-value-list-extent-requires-wildcard-coverage); the later inbound correction and maintained peer matrix establish the aggregate/value-list split recorded in [`SOURCES.md`](SOURCES.md).

## LF67 — the numeric-conversion host digit class is UTF-16-unit based, not Unicode-scalar based

> Date: 2026-07-23; extended 2026-07-30. Sections: §5, §6, and §7. Basis: [`spec/04-numbers-and-decimals.md` §4](../spec/04-numbers-and-decimals.md#4-other-numeric-constraints), [`spec/05-dates-and-time.md` §3](../spec/05-dates-and-time.md#3-constructing-dates-and-checking-validity); pinned kernel 30.8.1 `CheckEntityUtils`, Date-component `CheckBaseDateConstructionImpl.checkConstant`, Commons Lang 3.20.0 `NumberUtils.isParsable`, and `StringUtils.isNumeric`; read-only a12-dmkits conversion and Date-component controls at revisions `434f4ec8585797677d032639c50b491172bbbf54`, `2f97e4de6ff7f87c6d96b26b021763fda8a1f416`, and `f29dc2ad8a74acce6a9f8222262fabf8178a2bbf`; red/green Arabic-Indic, fullwidth, supplementary-plane, String-pattern, grammar, digit-budget, selected-domain, and Date-component cases; and the generalized validation/computation conversion laws. Accepted [`SPEC-2026-07-23-07`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-23-07--numeric-enumeration-conversion-uses-the-host-utf-16-digit-class) records the upstream wording/control result and the valid-model earlier-gate qualification; accepted [`SPEC-2026-07-27-11`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-27-11--date-component-constants-use-position-specific-width-and-range-gates) independently confirms the Date-component constant matrix.

“Host decimal digits” is not equivalent to either ASCII digits or every Unicode scalar with decimal category. Commons Lang iterates the Java `String` with `charAt` and calls `Character.isDigit(char)`. Arabic-Indic, fullwidth, and the other BMP decimal blocks are therefore admitted and mapped by decimal value, while a supplementary-plane decimal code point is presented as two surrogate `char` values and rejected. A code-point-wide port silently widens authoring even if all familiar non-ASCII examples remain green.

Lean pins the Java-21 BMP decimal-block profile and rejects every supplementary scalar at the same parser boundary. The shared host-decimal parser now has two completed semantic consumers: `FieldValueAsNumber` conversion and fixed quoted Date components. Checked String field sources additionally retain exact `[0-9]+` declaration metadata and are constrained to ASCII during declaration-owned ingestion, while Enumeration stored/category tokens and Date constants use the wider host profile. All feed their existing checked operands; missing, fixedness, scale, component position, generated validation, target checking, wrappers, and poison are unchanged. General Java-pattern compilation/execution remains injected, and pattern-bearing String targets continue to fail closed rather than borrowing this one executable declared-pattern profile. a12-dmkits revision `2f97e4de` externally locks Date-component BMP acceptance/decoding and supplementary rejection across both kernel strategies plus JVM/Node; revision `f29dc2ad` closes the remaining maintained mixed-source and exact range/width/empty rejection locks, so [`SPEC-2026-07-27-11`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-27-11--date-component-constants-use-position-specific-width-and-range-gates) is accepted.

## LF68 — filtered value-count polarity depends on match provenance, not filter presence alone

> Date: 2026-07-23. Sections: §5, §9, and §12. Basis: [`spec/04-numbers-and-decimals.md` §4](../spec/04-numbers-and-decimals.md#4-other-numeric-constraints); pinned kernel 30.8.1 `CheckAnzahlWertOpImpl`, `CheckEntityListenUtils`, and `RuntimeController.anzahlWertInFeldlisteIntern` at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; red/green filtered-match/non-match, empty-zero, scale-19 equality, computation, partial-relevance, and first-cause separators; trusted singleton/filter/unavailability laws; and the reviewed a12-dmkits handback at revision `a07409daeb69ad2d169c64ff1738c6fc22e0b3c4`. Pending [`SPEC-2026-07-23-12`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-23-12--filtered-value-counts-shrink-only-from-a-currently-selected-match) now owns only the peer's required reuse of the shared kept-successor traversal, not a semantic-result uncertainty.

`NumberOfValueInFields` does not share the ordinary aggregate shortcut “a reached filter makes every available result both-directionally fillable.” A filter always permits future growth because a currently excluded or changed value may become a match. Shrinkage requires a stronger fact: at least one value that currently contributes to the count was itself selected through that filter and may later disappear. A filtered selected non-match therefore yields grow-only uncertainty, while a filtered selected match yields both directions.

The amount scan still shares the established entity-list mechanics. It skips empty cells without substituting zero, compares Number through the scale-19 equality boundary, stops at the first formal cause, preserves authored slot order, and reuses hierarchical omitted tails plus computation's kept-successor traversal. What cannot be shared is the coarser `ResolvedValueListSide.hasHaving` summary by itself: after cells from multiple authored slots are appended, it no longer identifies which current matches came through a filter.

Lean therefore retains `selectedByHaving` on each `ResolvedValueCountCell` and keeps aggregate-wide filter/tail facts separately for growth. This is the minimum information an Execute or Analyze consumer needs to explain the directional result without reconstructing filter membership. The current checked consumer closes only the Number constant family over direct/plain-star/filtered-star Number entities in full validation, computation, and bounded partial validation. String/Enumeration/Boolean/Confirm typed admission, category and group expansion, shared numeric-expression/generated-validation/target integration, wider whole-rule orchestration, and retained external correspondence remain open.

## LF69 — computation traversal order can be real while its failure source is publicly cause-blind

> Date: 2026-07-24. Sections: §9 and §11. Basis: [`spec/07-repetition-and-iteration.md` §3](../spec/07-repetition-and-iteration.md#3-the-filter-having-the--correlation-and-aggregation), [`spec/09-computations.md` §3.2](../spec/09-computations.md#32-an-invalidity-clear-poisons), pinned kernel 30.8.1 `EbenenIterator`, generated Java/Groovy computation catches, and `DocumentComputationResultImpl` at revision `cb66e51fa7ab90b650698f861bf670754e2e1e66`; reviewed a12-dmkits shared traversal and controls at revision `f4642dfec13fcf79d37467a0d63710486d511c0d`; and accepted [`SPEC-2026-07-23-10`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-23-10--computation-having-prefetches-one-kept-successor-before-target-consumption) plus [`SPEC-2026-07-23-11`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-23-11--filtered-computation-value-aggregates-share-kept-successor-lookahead).

The one-kept-successor iterator fixes a real execution order: successor filters are searched before the pending current target is consumed. That order is publicly observable when a prefix consumer such as `FirstFilledValue` terminates before some suffix, because a wrong traversal changes whether the suffix is reached at all. A draining value aggregate instead reaches the same filter and target failure set under the competing eager-filter and kept-successor accounts.

The kernel's generated computation catches discard `CalculationFieldException` source identity and mark only the computed target as erroneous; the V2 public result likewise exposes the erroneous computed instance rather than the upstream poison source. Consequently two draining traversals that fail from different first sources can project to the same public outcome. Internal Lean dependency outcomes may retain richer causes for proofs, explanation, and deterministic scheduling, but kernel compatibility must not claim those causes as public observables without separate evidence. Separating matrices need one consumer with teeth: use prefix reachability for the public discriminator and source inspection plus shared-owner guards for the otherwise cause-blind draining route.

Composition-triage label (2026-07-25): the prefix-reachability half is `confirmed-probe` through accepted [`SPEC-2026-07-23-10`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-23-10--computation-having-prefetches-one-kept-successor-before-target-consumption) and [`SPEC-2026-07-23-11`](archived/A12-DMKITS-SPEC-SYNC-LEDGER-THROUGH-2026-08-28.md#spec-2026-07-23-11--filtered-computation-value-aggregates-share-kept-successor-lookahead); the cause-identity half is **knowingly unobservable** and must not be re-triaged or scheduled for a probe. Retaining richer internal causes stays legitimate for proofs, explanation, and deterministic scheduling, but no kernel-correspondence claim may be made for them.

## LF70 — a defensive re-check is dead only when the caller owns the fact it re-checks

> Date: 2026-07-24. Sections: §9 and §10. Basis: this repository's own checked-lowering sources at revision `6726588`; no external kernel observation is involved, because the finding is about the formalization's certificate chain rather than about A12 behavior.
